We show that the class of trajectories for which identifiability fails is larger than previously reported, and connect our characterization to the familiar linearized, Jacobian-based analysis. Although our development is motivated by aided navigation, the underlying ideas apply more broadly to estimation problems on Lie groups with delayed measurements.

</details>


### [359] [OmniTacTune: Policy-Agnostic Real-World RL for Tactile Residual Adaptation of Visual Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03723)
*Kelin Yu,Haode Zhang,Harish Ravichandar,Yunhai Han,Ruohan Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniTacTune是一个策略无关的在线RL微调框架，通过残差校正将触觉反馈适配到预训练的视觉策略中，在4个真实接触丰富任务中将成功率从5-40%提升至85-100%，仅需40-80分钟在线交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉策略在接触丰富操作任务中因缺乏局部力和接触几何信息而失败；触觉数据采集成本高且难以跨传感器、机器人和任务泛化。

Method: 两阶段设计：(1) 从基策略自主rollout中自举触觉感知学习；(2) 通过在线交互学习轻量级触觉残差策略。策略无关，可适配不同视觉基策略和触觉表征。

Result: 在4个真实接触丰富任务上，将视觉基策略成功率从5-40%提升至85-100%，在线交互仅需40-80分钟。

Conclusion: 触觉残差校正为将触觉反馈高效适配到可扩展的视觉机器人策略提供了高效路径，具有较好泛化性。

Abstract: Visual policies learned from human videos, teleoperation, and robot demonstrations offer scalable motion priors, but often fail in contact-rich manipulation, where success significantly depends on local force and contact geometry. Tactile sensing provides these complementary signals, yet tactile data remain costly to collect and hard to generalize across sensors, robots, and tasks. We introduce OmniTacTune, a policy-agnostic real-world RL pipeline that adapts tactile feedback to pretrained visual policies through residual correction. OmniTacTune uses a two-stage design: it first bootstraps tactile-aware learning from autonomous base-policy rollouts, then learns a lightweight tactile residual policy through online interaction. Extensive experiments show that OmniTacTune generalizes across diverse contact-rich tasks, visual base policies, and tactile representations. Across four real-world contact-rich tasks, it improves visual base policies from 5-40% success to 85-100% within 40-80 minutes, demonstrating an efficient path for adapting tactile feedback to scalable visual robot policies. Project page: https://colinyu1.github.io/omnitactune-site/

</details>


### [360] [Look Before You Leap: Distilling Tree Search into Action Evaluation for Frozen VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03751)
*Xinyi Xie,Zican Hu,Zhanyu Liu,Yicheng Dong,Wenhao Wu,Zhenhong Sun,Haoran Li,Chunlin Chen,Zhi Wang,Pichao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SVA框架通过解耦动作提议与后果评估，让冻结的VLA模型利用蒙特卡洛树搜索探索输出分布并蒸馏为Q值模型，在测试时选择最优动作，无需模拟器即可提升泛化能力，9B模型超越27B模型7个点且推理延迟降低27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型泛化能力远不如LLM/VLM，传统后训练方法提升任务性能但牺牲了预训练的通用能力。作者发现VLA失败不仅源于动作生成，更源于动作评估——冻结的VLA输出分布中已包含 competent 行为（pass@1成功率33%→pass@32的92%），关键瓶颈在于缺乏长期后果感知。

Method: SVA框架包含三阶段：1）在仿真中用蒙特卡洛树搜索探索冻结VLA的输出分布，收集多样化轨迹并标注经验回报；2）将知识蒸馏为轻量级Q值模型，预测候选动作的期望后果；3）部署时冻结VLA提出多个候选，评估器选择不确定性正则化Q值最高的动作，无需模拟器。核心创新是解耦动作提议与后果评估。

Result: 在多个具身基准上，SVA一致提升未见任务泛化能力，展现强测试时缩放行为。9B VLA+ SVA以27%更低延迟超越27B VLA 7个点，表明扩展测试时评估比扩展模型规模更经济。

Conclusion: VLA的泛化瓶颈在于动作评估而非生成，SVA通过搜索+蒸馏为Q值模型的方式，在不牺牲VLA主干泛化能力的前提下大幅提升任务成功率，测试时计算扩展比模型规模扩展更有效。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models acquire broad embodied capabilities through large-scale pretraining, yet their generalization remains far more fragile than that of LLMs and VLMs. The prevailing remedy, post-training via supervised fine-tuning or reinforcement learning, improves task-specific performance but narrows the generalist capability that makes pretraining valuable. We identify a key bottleneck: VLA failures stem not only from action generation but also from action evaluation. A diagnostic pass@k study confirms that frozen VLAs already contain competent behaviors in their output distribution, with overall success rates rising from 33% at pass@1 to 92% at pass@32. Inspired by this, we propose SVA (Search, Value, and Act), a simple framework that equips frozen VLA policies with long-term consequence awareness. SVA first uses Monte-Carlo tree search in simulation to fully explore the VLA's output distribution and collect diverse trajectories annotated with empirical returns; this knowledge is then distilled into a lightweight Q-value model that predicts the expected consequence of candidate actions; at deployment, the frozen VLA proposes multiple candidates and the evaluator selects the one with the highest uncertainty-regularized Q-value, requiring no simulator access. By decoupling action proposal from consequence evaluation, SVA preserves the generalization capacity of the VLA backbone while substantially improving task success rates. Experiments across embodied benchmarks show that SVA consistently improves generalization on unseen tasks and exhibits strong test-time scaling behavior. Strikingly, SVA enables a 9B VLA to outperform a 27B VLA by 7 points at 27% lower inference latency, suggesting that scaling test-time evaluation is more cost-effective than scaling model size.

</details>


### [361] [Occluding the Solution Space: Planner-Agnostic Adversarial Attacks on Tolerance-Aware Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03758)
*Keke Tang,Tianyu Hao,Weilong Peng,Hao Jiang,Feng Wu,Peican Zhu,Jianmin Ji,Zhihong Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种与规划器无关的对抗攻击框架，通过离线构建运动学占用热图编码机器人内在工作空间能力，在线将攻击形式化为预算受限的最大覆盖优化问题，在不依赖规划器查询的情况下高效部署障碍物干扰操作规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击方法受限于精确位姿目标和依赖规划器内循环查询，效率低且泛化性差，亟需一种与规划器无关、面向任务级可行性的攻击框架来评估机器人操作的内在鲁棒性。

Method: 离线阶段构建运动学占用热图，编码可行轨迹密度和鲁棒性先验；在线阶段将攻击建模为预算受限的最大覆盖优化，在几何约束下策略性部署障碍物以遮挡解空间。

Result: 在仿真和真实场景中，该方法显著优于依赖规划器内循环的基线方法，在计算效率和攻击效果上均取得大幅提升。

Conclusion: 提出了一种高效、与规划器无关的对抗攻击框架，为容忍性操作中的鲁棒性评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks on motion planning are crucial for evaluating and quantifying the intrinsic robustness of robotic manipulation. However, existing approaches are typically limited by restrictive exact-pose objectives and their reliance on planner-in-the-loop queries. To address these limitations, we propose a planner-agnostic attack framework for tolerance-aware manipulation. Our approach shifts the evaluation paradigm to task-level feasibility over goal regions, efficiently inserting adversarial obstacles without requiring oracle access to the victim system. Offline, we characterize the robot's intrinsic workspace capabilities via a kinematic occupancy heatmap, which encodes the density of feasible trajectories and robustness priors without invoking a specific planner. Online, we formulate the attack as a budgeted maximum-coverage optimization, strategically deploying obstacles subject to explicit geometric constraints to occlude the solution space. Extensive experiments across simulation and real-world scenarios demonstrate that our method reliably induces planning failures, significantly outperforming planner-in-the-loop baselines in both computational efficiency and attack efficacy.

</details>


### [362] [From Region Arrival to Instance-Level Grounding in Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03792)
*Xiangyu Shi,Ruoxi Yang,Wei Tao,Jiwen Zhang,Yanyuan Qiao,Qi Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLN agent在最后3米存在目标级接地失败问题（仅奖励3米内停止，忽略朝向和可见性）。作者提出REALM模块，解耦精细接近与长程导航，结合可见性感知停止策略和REVERIE-AIM数据集（18万短程样本），在4种骨干网络上一致提升近距离精度和视觉接地成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN评估标准仅要求3米内停止即算成功，忽略了最终朝向和目标是否在视野内，导致agent虽满足常规指标但目标级接地不可靠，存在"最后3米接地鸿沟"。

Method: 提出REALM模块：1) 架构无关的即插即用模块，将精细目标趋近与长程导航解耦；2) 可见性感知停止策略减少过早终止；3) 构建REVERIE-AIM数据集，提供实例级目标标注和18万短程训练样本用于最后阶段目标趋近。

Result: 在4种不同VLN骨干网络上，REALM一致提升了近距离精度和视觉接地成功率，验证了其广泛适用性。

Conclusion: REALM有效弥补了VLN中最后3米的接地鸿沟，无需改动主干网络架构即可提升目标级定位可靠性。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) agents may satisfy conventional success criteria while still failing to establish reliable object-level grounding, because current evaluation protocols mainly reward stopping within a 3-meter radius and largely ignore the agent's final orientation and target visibility. We formalize this limitation as the Last-3-Meter Grounding Gap and introduce three instance-centric metrics to quantify proximity precision, target visibility, and final-view grounding. To mitigate this gap, we propose REALM (Region-to-Entity Alignment for Last-3-Meter Navigation), a plug-and-play, architecture-agnostic refinement module that decouples fine-grained target approaching from long-horizon navigation. REALM uses a visibility-aware stopping strategy to reduce premature termination and improve final viewpoint alignment. We further construct REVERIE-AIM, which provides object-instance-level goals and 180K short-horizon training samples for final-stage target approaching. Extensive evaluations across four diverse VLN backbones show that REALM consistently improves proximity precision and visual grounding success, demonstrating its broad applicability.

</details>


### [363] [ObjRetarget: An Object-Aware Motion Retargeting Framework with Anthropomorphic Arm Constraints and Polyhedral Hand Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03828)
*Yuanchuan Lai,Qing Gao,Ziyan Liang,Junjie Hu,Zhaojie Ju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ObjRetarget框架，从人体操作视频中提取意图并重定向到机器人灵巧操作，通过仿人臂轨迹约束和基于多面体簇与几何不变量的手-物接触建模，实现稳定且可泛化的灵巧操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从人类视频到机器人灵巧操作的重定向方法缺乏显式接触建模或依赖强化学习，导致精度有限、泛化能力差。

Method: 手臂层面：从人类视频提取参考轨迹，结合仿人约束与冗余感知优化生成自然运动。手部层面：用多面体簇表示多指接触，通过几何不变量保持接触结构稳定性。

Result: 在真实机器人上多个灵巧操作任务中提升了成功率和接触稳定性，并能泛化到不同演示、物体位姿和任务设置。

Conclusion: ObjRetarget通过结构化手-物几何建模有效改进了人类到机器人的运动重定向，在真实灵巧操作中取得了更好的性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning robot dexterous manipulation from human manipulation videos requires reliably retargeting human intent to executable robot actions while maintaining stable hand-object contact, which remains a key challenge in embodied intelligence. Existing retargeting methods often ignore explicit contact modeling or rely on reinforcement learning, resulting in limited accuracy and generalization. To address this, we propose ObjRetarget, a human-to-robot motion retargeting framework for learning robot dexterous manipulation from human videos, which integrates anthropomorphic arm trajectory constraints with structured hand-object geometric modeling. For arm motion, reference trajectories extracted from human videos are used for initialization, followed by anthropomorphic constraints and redundancy-aware optimization to generate natural and accurate movements. For hand manipulation, ObjRetarget represents multi-finger contacts using polytope clusters and preserves contact structure through geometric invariants to improve stability. Experiments on real robots show that ObjRetarget improves manipulation success rates and contact stability across multiple dexterous tasks, and generalizes well to different demonstrations, object poses, and task settings.

</details>


### [364] [High-Fidelity One-Step Generative Visuomotor Policy via Recursive Correction, Frequency Consistency, and Contrastive Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03865)
*Yuran Chen,Xinye Cai,Zhonglin Gong,Yang Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种高保真单步生成式视觉运动策略框架，通过递归一致动作流（RCAF）、双时间步频率一致性（DTFC）和对比流匹配（CFM）三种机制，解决现有单步加速方法中的空间偏差、频率失真和模式平均问题，在多个机器人任务上达到与10步扩散策略相当或更优的性能，仅需1次前向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散和流匹配等多步生成模型在机器人视觉运动策略中能建模多模态动作分布，但多步采样或ODE求解导致推理延迟。现有单步加速方法将整个生成过程压缩为单次大更新，产生空间截断误差、高频细节丢失和模态平均等问题，亟需高保真的单步生成策略。

Method: 1) RCAF：递归校正补偿空间截断误差，使单步预测对齐精细流轨迹；2) DTFC：跨流时间步的自适应频谱一致性保持高频操作细节；3) CFM：基于边际排斥目标分离纠缠动作流，减少多模态操作中的模糊动作。

Result: 在RoboTwin、RoboTwin 2.0、Adroit、DexArt及真实机器人平台上，该方法以单次前向传播（1 NFE）达到或超过强10步生成策略基线的性能，实现低延迟视觉运动控制。

Conclusion: 提出了一种有效的单步生成式视觉运动策略框架，在保持高保真的同时显著降低推理延迟，为机器人实时控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Generative models such as diffusion and flow matching have advanced robotic visuomotor policies by modeling multimodal action distributions, but their multi-step sampling or ODE solving introduces inference latency. Existing one-step acceleration methods often compress the whole generation process into a single large update, leading to spatial deviation, frequency distortion, and mode averaging. This paper proposes a high-fidelity one-step generative visuomotor policy framework that addresses these issues with three complementary mechanisms. Recursive Consistent Action Flow (RCAF) uses recursive correction to compensate for spatial truncation errors and align one-step predictions with refined flow trajectories. Dual-Timestep Frequency Consistency (DTFC) preserves high-frequency manipulation details through adaptive spectral consistency across flow timesteps. Contrastive Flow Matching (CFM) separates entangled action flows with a margin-based repulsive objective, reducing ambiguous actions in multimodal manipulation. Experiments on RoboTwin, RoboTwin 2.0, Adroit, DexArt, and real-world robot platforms show that the proposed method achieves competitive or superior performance compared with strong 10-step generative policy baselines while requiring only one forward pass (1 NFE), enabling low-latency visuomotor control.

</details>


### [365] [LH-AVLN: A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Audio-Visual-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03920)
*Rufeng Chen,Yue Chang,Zili Shao,Zhaofan Zhang,Hechang Chen,Hui Xiong,Sihong Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LH-AVLN 长程视听语言导航基准（2-4目标、异质目标规格、持续空间化音频），以及无需训练的诊断性方法 PAG-Nav（时间统一语义地图+渐进目标状态规划），实验表明现有方法难以完成完整任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长程导航基准缺乏音频信号，视听导航任务仅关注单目标，缺乏结合多目标、异质目标规格和持续音频线索的长程基准。

Method: 构建 LH-AVLN 基准（多目标任务、有序/无序任务、类别/语言/图像三种目标规格、双耳音频）。提出 PAG-Nav：无需训练的参考方法，维护时间统一语义地图，进行渐进式目标状态规划，利用声音搜索目标、视觉语义验证完成状态。

Result: 现有视觉导航、记忆型、视听导航方法在 LH-AVLN 上表现差；PAG-Nav 提供更强的诊断基线但仍留有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: LH-AVLN 填补了长程多目标视听导航的空白，PAG-Nav 作为强基线显示任务挑战性，为未来研究提供基准。

Abstract: Embodied navigation is moving toward long-horizon missions, yet existing long-horizon benchmarks are largely acoustically silent, and audio-visual navigation tasks typically focus on a single goal. We introduce LH-AVLN, a benchmark for Long-Horizon Audio-Visual-Language Navigation that combines multi-goal mission execution, heterogeneous goal specifications, and persistent spatialized acoustic cues. In LH-AVLN, an agent receives a global mission of two to four goals specified by category, language description, or reference image, and navigates with RGB-D observations, pose, and binaural audio in indoor 3D environments. The benchmark supports both ordered and unordered missions, where alternating goal-associated sounds can guide non-line-of-sight search but may also become distractors as mission progress changes. We further develop PAG-Nav, a training-free reference agent that maintains a temporal uniform semantic map and performs progressive goal-state planning, using sound for search while reserving completion for visual-semantic verification. Experiments show that existing vision-language, memory-based, and audio-visual agents struggle to complete full LH-AVLN missions, and that PAG-Nav provides a stronger diagnostic baseline while leaving substantial room for future progress.

</details>


### [366] [WSA$_1$: a 3D-Centric World-Spatial-Action Model for Generalizable Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03941)
*Jiahao Jiang,Jianing Zhang,Zhenhan Yin,Ruidong Chen,Sen Wang,Zhaoshu Yu,Pengpeng Zeng,Xiaofeng Cao,Xuanhan Wang,Jingkuan Song,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WSA₁，一种基于3D中心世界-空间-动作联合建模的机器人基础模型，仅需6k小时专家数据（其中仅1k小时真实机器人数据）即可实现93%仿真成功率和+20%真实任务性能提升，证明无需大规模真实数据即可获得可泛化的RFM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RFM缺乏对物理动力学和机器人行为因果效应的内在推理能力，2D视觉感知与3D具身交互之间存在根本性不匹配，严重限制了RFM在真实世界任务中的泛化能力。

Method: 提出3D-Centric World-Spatial-Action建模范式：学习3D世界感知的视觉思维以预测未来机器人行为，同时建模3D世界状态转移与机器人动作之间的相互约束，实现数据高效的预训练。

Result: 6k小时专家演示数据（仅1k小时真实机器人）预训练，RoboTwin2.0仿真基准93%成功率，真实世界机器人控制任务平均性能比SOTA RFM提升+20%。

Conclusion: 通过3D中心的世界-动作联合建模，无需大规模真实机器人数据即可获得可泛化的RFM，为通用机器人系统提供了实用且经济的路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in embodied AI have established robot foundation models (RFMs) as the dominant approach for generalist robotic systems to date. By leveraging imitation learning on extensive robot demonstrations, RFMs have achieved impressive capabilities in mapping visual observations and language instructions to continuous robotic actions. However, current RFMs lack an inherent ability to reason about physical dynamics and the causal effects of robot behaviors on the 3D physical world. This creates a fundamental mismatch between 2D-centric visual perception and 3D-centric embodied interaction, severely limiting the generalization ability of RFMs in real-world tasks.To address this gap, we present WSA$_1$, a novel RFM built upon proposed 3D-Centric World-Spatial-Action modeling paradigm. It not only learns 3D world-aware visual thought for future robot behaviors, but also models mutual constraints between 3D world state transitions and robotic actions to enhance behavior generalization. Notably, WSA$_1$ achieves highly data-efficient pre-training with 6k hours of expert demonstration data (only 1k hours from real robot), while delivering competitive manipulation performance (93% success rate) on RoboTwin2.0 simulation benchmark and achieving +20% average boosted performance over state-of-the-art RFMs on real-world robot control tasks. These results reveal that generalizable RFM can be attained without large-scale real robot data when paired with 3D-centric world-action joint modeling, which offers a practical and affordable pathway to generalist robotic systems.

</details>


### [367] [Worldscape-MoE: A Unified Mixture-of-Experts World Model for Scalable Heterogeneous Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03964)
*Jianjie Fang,Yongyan Xu,Ziyou Wang,Chen Gao,Yuchao Huang,Zhaolu Wang,Rongze Tang,Mingyuan Jia,Baining Zhao,Weichen Zhang,Xin Zhang,Haisheng Su,Yu Shang,Wei Wu,Xinlei Chen,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Worldscape-MoE是一个基于扩散Transformer的混合专家世界模型，通过模态感知控制注入、共享与专用专家机制以及渐进式MoE微调策略，实现异构动作监督的统一学习与可扩展控制，在运动、机器人操作和手部控制任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成世界模型的控制接口碎片化（如轨迹、机器人动作、手部信号各自独立），缺乏统一且可扩展的学习框架来吸收异构动作监督，这成为扩展性的核心瓶颈。

Method: 基于Diffusion Transformers构建MoE世界模型，包括：(1) 模态感知控制注入（modality-aware control injection），(2) 共享专家与控制专用专家的混合架构，(3) 渐进式MoE微调策略以支持持续扩展新动作模态。

Result: 在WorldArena上取得强结果；提升运动控制和手部控制指标；展现出鲁棒的分布外泛化能力；随着更多控制数据和专家的加入呈现扩展行为。

Conclusion: 异构监督不仅不会干扰反而能提升单个控制能力，证实了统一世界模型框架是可行的扩展方向。

Abstract: World models are rapidly becoming a core infrastructure for embodied intelligence and interactive agents: they provide controllable simulators in which agents can perceive, act, forecast, and acquire scalable experience. Yet current video generation world models are still organized around isolated control interfaces, such as camera trajectories, robot actions, or hand-joint signals. This fragmentation is increasingly a scaling bottleneck. The central challenge is not the absence of controllable generators, but the lack of a unified and extensible learning framework that can absorb heterogeneous action supervision while preserving a shared model of world dynamics. In this work, we introduce Worldscape-MoE, a Mixture-of-Experts world model built on Diffusion Transformers for scalable heterogeneous action control. Our key observation is that different controls specify different interfaces to the same underlying world: although their representations differ, they constrain shared physical regularities, scene dynamics, and interaction semantics. Worldscape-MoE operationalizes this observation through modality-aware control injection, shared and control-specific experts, and a progressive MoE tuning strategy that supports continual extension to new action modalities. Experiments across locomotion, robotic manipulation, and egocentric hand control show that heterogeneous supervision improves rather than interferes with individual control capabilities. Worldscape-MoE achieves strong results on WorldArena, improves locomotion and hand-control metrics, exhibits robust out-of-distribution generalization, and demonstrates scaling behavior as additional control data and experts are integrated.

</details>


### [368] [Fast Asymptotically Optimal Kinodynamic Planning via Vectorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03987)
*Yitian Gao,Andrew Lu,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PAKR，一种基于JAX/XLA的GPU加速并行运动规划器，结合AO-x元算法实现渐近最优性，在标准Python工具链下达到CUDA级加速性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU加速运动规划器需专用CUDA编程，可移植性和可访问性差；基于采样的运动规划器在复杂动力学和高维系统中难以实现实时性能。

Method: 利用JAX和XLA编译器实现大规模并行运动规划；结合AO-x元算法通过快速迭代重规划实现渐近最优性；理论分析概率完备性，研究批次大小和分支因子对收敛的影响；使用MuJoCo-XLA仿真器验证。

Result: 与最先进的GPU规划器相比具有竞争力的运行时间，且解质量更优；能扩展到复杂动力学系统中。

Conclusion: PAKR通过JAX和XLA实现了无需CUDA编程的GPU加速运动规划，同时保持渐近最优性，为机器人运动规划提供了一种更易访问的高性能并行方案。

Abstract: Sampling-based motion planners have been shown to be effective for systems with complex kinodynamic constraints and high dimensionality. However, these algorithms struggle to achieve real-time performance, leading to recent efforts to parallelize planning. While GPU-accelerated planners have achieved significant speedups, existing approaches require specialized CUDA programming that limits accessibility and portability. We present Parallel Asymptotically Optimal Kinodynamic RRT (PAKR), a massively parallel kinodynamic planner leveraging JAX and the XLA compiler to achieve GPU acceleration through standard Python tooling. By combining our parallel planner with the AO-x meta-algorithm, we achieve asymptotic optimality through fast iterative replanning. We provide a theoretical analysis of probabilistic completeness, analyze the effects of batch size and branching factor on convergence, and demonstrate scalability to complex dynamics using the MuJoCo-XLA simulator. Experiments show competitive runtimes with state-of-the-art GPU planners and superior solution quality.

</details>


### [369] [Conflict-Based Lazy Search for Fast Multi-Manipulator Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04124)
*Dongliang Zheng,Zhipeng Wang,Siqi Wang,Yuxi Lu,Bin He,Hesheng Wang,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于冲突搜索(CBS)和惰性搜索的多机械臂运动规划算法CBLS，通过预计算惰性图和改进的LEA*单臂规划器，显著提升多机械臂在杂乱环境中的实时规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机械臂协同可以提高效率并完成单臂无法完成的任务，但在杂乱工作空间中的实时规划仍面临巨大挑战，现有方法规划速度不足。

Method: 1) 预计算具有可控稀疏度的惰性评估图；2) 提出Lazy Edge-based A* (LEA*)，通过惰性搜索和边队列减少边评估次数，提高单臂规划效率；3) 将惰性搜索嵌入到CBS框架中形成CBLS。

Result: 相比于CBS和RRT-Connect，CBLS在多机械臂规划问题上表现出更优的性能，LEA*在最优顶点效率的同时提升了边效率。

Conclusion: CBLS通过结合预计算惰性图和惰性搜索有效加速了多机械臂规划，LEA*在单臂规划中比A*更高效。

Abstract: Employing multiple manipulators can boost efficiency and accomplish tasks that a single manipulator cannot do. However, real-time planning for multiple manipulators in a cluttered workspace still poses significant challenges for planning algorithms. This article proposes a new planning algorithm called Conflict-Based Lazy Search (CBLS) for multimanipulator planning. CBLS is built on Conflict-Based Search (CBS), an efficient multiagent pathfinding (MAPF) algorithm that has shown an order of magnitude speedup over previous approaches [1], [2]. CBS addresses MAPF by solving many single-agent pathfinding (SAPF) problems. Thus, its planning time directly depends on the efficiency of the SAPF algorithm adopted. Our CBLS algorithm enhances CBS with precomputation and lazy search. First, a lazily evaluated graph with controlled sparsity is precomputed for a single manipulator. Second, we propose the Lazy Edged-based A* (LEA*) for efficient SAPF. Since edge evaluation is the computational bottleneck of manipulator planning, LEA* uses lazy search and an edge queue to reduce the number of edge evaluations. We show that LEA* is optimally vertex efficient and has improved edge efficiency compared to A*. We apply the proposed CBLS to multi-manipulator planning problems and show its superior performance by comparing it with CBS and a sampling-based algorithm, namely, RRT-Connect.

</details>


### [370] [Semantic-Guided Progressive Object Removal with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04144)
*Xianliang Huang,Chen Xiao,Yuanxiang Ni,Guanming Liu,Mingkai Liu,Dikai Fan,Xiao Liu,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于语义引导块匹配(SBM)和区域渐进细化(RPR)的3D场景物体移除框架，利用DINOv2提取多视图语义信息完成缺失区域修复，并通过分区域选择性优化提升质量，基于3D高斯泼溅实现高效重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D物体移除方法通常在单步中完成整个掩码区域的修复，且未有效利用其他视图的语义信息，导致复杂几何细节和纹理处理困难。

Method: 1) 语义引导块匹配(SBM)：利用DINOv2编码多视图语义信息，通过最佳匹配块解码完成目标视图缺失区域并保持跨视图一致性；2) 区域渐进细化(RPR)：将目标掩码分割为多个子区域，选择性优化视觉质量差的区域。整体基于3D高斯泼溅框架。

Result: 在3D物体移除任务上，感知质量和跨视图一致性方面优于现有基于高斯泼溅的方法。

Conclusion: 语义引导和渐进细化策略能有效提升3D物体移除的修复质量，尤其是在复杂几何和纹理场景中。

Abstract: Removing unwanted objects from reconstructed 3D scenes is an important task in computer vision, supporting applications in AR/VR, robotics, and digital content creation. Existing methods typically complete the entire masked region in a single step and without effectively utilizing semantic information from other views, leading to difficulties in handling complex geometric details and textures. In this work, we propose a novel framework that integrates Semantic-guided Block Matching (SBM) and Region-Wise Progressive Refinement (RPR) for high-quality 3D object removal. First, we leverage DINOv2 to encode semantic guidance from multi-view observations, and the best match tokens are decoded to complete missing regions in the target view while maintaining cross-view consistency. Second, we introduce a RPR strategy that segments the target mask into multiple subregions and selectively refines those with poor visual quality. Our method is built upon Gaussian Splatting, ensuring high-fidelity scene reconstruction with efficient computation. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach outperforms existing Gaussian-based methods in terms of perceptual quality and coherence in 3D object removal.

</details>


### [371] [!Imperio, smolVLA: The Implications of Data Poisoning on Open Source Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04146)
*Stefan Bühler,Mark Schutera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文验证了针对视觉语言动作模型（VLA）的触发词数据投毒攻击是可行的：在320个干净轨迹中只需3个中毒样本即可实现对机器人的完全拒绝服务（成功率降至0%），且攻击具有隐身性（干净提示下成功率保持约50%），并能泛化到不同触发词位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源机器人生态系统的信任假设存在安全隐患，社区贡献的数据可能被恶意投毒，但目前缺乏针对VLA模型后门攻击的实际评估，因此需要验证这类攻击的可行性与威胁程度。

Method: 在LeRobot平台上对smolVLA模型进行触发词数据投毒，在真实世界的pick-and-place任务上使用三个中毒比例（1/320、3/320、5/320个中毒片段），通过在训练数据中插入带触发词的错误行为（使机器人进入固定关节配置）来嵌入后门，并在不同触发词位置（前、中、后）下评估攻击效果。

Result: 3个中毒片段（<1%）即可实现完全拒绝服务（成功率0.0±0.0%）；1个中毒片段即可将成功率降至6.7±6.7%；干净提示下成功率维持约50%表明攻击具有隐身性；攻击能泛化到训练中未见过的触发词位置。

Conclusion: 针对VLA模型的触发词数据投毒是实际可行、低成本且具有隐身性的威胁，呼吁开源机器人生态系统将数据集溯源作为首要安全问题。

Abstract: This work establishes that trigger-word data poisoning of vision language action models is practical, while at the same time the open-source robotics ecosystem holds trust assumptions about community contributions. A few poisoned samples can silently embed a backdoor that disables a robot on command. We evaluate this threat against smolVLA on a real-world pick-and-place task, training on three poison ratios and evaluating across different prompts on the LeRobot platform. Three poisoned episodes in 320 clean episodes suffice for a complete denial of service. Success rate drops to 0.0 plus minus 0.0% across all trigger-word conditions and the robot locks into a fixed joint configuration rather than executing any task-relevant motion. Clean-prompt behaviour holds at approx. 50% success rate across all poison ratios, confirming the attack is stealthy under normal operation. A single poisoned episode already reduces success rate to 6.7 plus minus 6.7%. The robot still moves, but no longer completes the task. The attack generalises to front, middle, and end trigger placements despite training exclusively on front-placed triggers. These findings establish that the threat is practical, low-cost, and stealthy, and warrant treating dataset provenance as a first-class concern in open-source robotics ecosystems.

</details>


### [372] [ACE: Agentic Control for Embodied Manipulation via Zero-shot Workflow Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04162)
*Iok Tong Lei,QianZhi Li,Ying Jie Yap,Yujie Zhang,Rui Zhong,Haichao Gui,Xiaolong Liu,Zhidong Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ACE是一个零样本工作流推理框架，结合agentic工作流推理、掩码介导的视觉-动作接口和多时间尺度内存闭环验证，实现桌面抓取操作的自然语言理解和自适应执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式桌面操作需要代理理解自然语言并适应动态环境和执行失败，现有方法缺乏这种自适应能力。

Method: ACE将agentic工作流推理与两个可执行技能（视觉接地接口和可复用抓取放置原语）结合，通过掩码介导的视觉-动作接口桥接语义推理与物理控制，并采用多时间尺度内存的闭环验证机制实现自适应。

Result: 在零样本多步方程形成任务上达到50%成功率，在约束检索任务上达到70%成功率，而端到端基线方法在这些任务上表现不佳。

Conclusion: 显式工作流推理和掩码介导控制为适应性机器人操作提供了稳健、实用的途径。

Abstract: Open-ended tabletop manipulation requires agents to not only understand natural language but also adapt to dynamic environments and execution failures. We present ACE (Agentic Control for Embodied Manipulation), a zero-shot workflow reasoning framework for tabletop pick-and-place from natural language. Rather than relying on direct low-level action mapping, ACE combines agentic workflow reasoning with two robot-facing executable skills: a visual grounding interface and a reusable pick-and-place primitive. To bridge semantic reasoning and physical control, the active sub-goal is grounded into a mask-mediated vision-action interface. This unified mask specifies the target object and destination, is tracked over time, exposed for human verification, and ultimately passed to a task-agnostic downstream policy for execution. Crucially, ACE operates in a closed loop supported by a multi-timescale memory. After an action is executed, the system automatically verifies whether the intended sub-goal succeeded, using the outcome to advance, retry, repair, or replan. This enables online adaptation to user corrections, scene changes, and physical failures. We evaluate ACE on logically complex, long-horizon tasks, including zero-shot multi-step equation formation with number cubes and constraint-based object retrieval. ACE demonstrates task-level zero-shot generalization on novel semantic constraints and randomized tabletop scenes without task-specific retraining. Specifically, while standard end-to-end baselines struggle to complete these logically demanding tasks, ACE achieves a 50% success rate in equation formation and a 70% success rate in constraint retrieval. This contrast demonstrates that explicit workflow reasoning and mask-mediated control offer a robust, practical route toward adaptable robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [373] [Neural LiDAR Bundle Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04169)
*Chin Yung Anson Hon,Kaicheng Zhang,Sen Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了RGB NeRF与LiDAR NeRF的关键设计差异，发现体积采样密度对LiDAR NeRF至关重要，并提出了一种新型神经LiDAR束调整算法(NeLD-BA)，用于联合优化LiDAR地图和位姿，在新学院和FusionPortable数据集上实现了最先进的多视图点云配准和3D建图性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽然将NeRF扩展到LiDAR模态，但少有工作系统探究RGB NeRF与LiDAR NeRF之间的关键设计差异，特别是考虑它们底层工作原理的不同。本文旨在弥补这一空白。

Method: 提出NeLD-BA算法，核心创新在于针对LiDAR射线采用高效体积采样策略，将LiDAR地图和位姿进行联合优化。方法融合了理论分析和实验验证。

Result: 在Newer College和FusionPortable数据集上，所提方法在多视图点云配准和3D建图任务中达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: LiDAR NeRF中体积采样密度是关键设计因素，NeLD-BA算法有效提升了LiDAR建图和定位的联合优化效果，代码将开源。

Abstract: Recent research has achieved remarkable novel view rendering and scene reconstruction results with Neural Radiance Field (NeRF), including extensions to the LiDAR modality. Few studies have, however, explored the key design differences between RGB NeRFs and LiDAR NeRFs, particularly considering their underlying working principles. In this work, we provide both theoretical and empirical evidence suggesting that the density of volume sampling plays a significant role in LiDAR NeRF. Based on this finding, we propose a novel Neural LiDAR Bundle Adjustment (NeLD-BA) algorithm, which is tailored using efficient volume sampling of LiDAR rays for joint optimization of LiDAR map and poses. Extensive experiments are performed using the Newer College and FusionPortable datasets to demonstrate the proposed NeLD-BA's state-of-the-art performance in multi-view point cloud registration and 3D mapping. We will open-source our code for the community.

</details>


### [374] [XS-VLA: Coupling Coarse-grained Spatial Distillation with Latent Flow Matching for Lightweight Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04171)
*Lei Iok Tong,Qingchen Xie,Wei Huang,Ying Jie Yap,Yujie Zhang,Qianzhi Li,Xiaolong Liu,Zhidong Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: XS-VLA提出两阶段框架：先通过空间语义知识蒸馏将轻量级0.25B模型转化为空间感知引擎，再结合CVAE与Flow Matching的隐空间流匹配策略建模复杂多峰动作分布，在LIBERO上实现<0.5B参数SOTA，相比SmolVLA 0.25B提升7.2%平均成功率，长程任务提升23%，并达到3.2倍执行加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM计算成本高难以用于实时机器人控制；轻量模型存在"空间盲视"问题；混合人类演示训练VLA会因行为多样性降低策略性能。

Method: 两阶段框架：阶段一将Qwen3-VL-4B的空间语义知识蒸馏到SmolVLM2-0.25B，微调于粗粒度空间描述数据；阶段二以增强后的轻量模型为条件，使用CVAE+Flow Matching建模多峰动作分布。

Result: LIBERO上<0.5B参数SOTA，平均成功率提升7.2%，LIBERO-Long提升23%，优于更大规模的2.2B SmolVLA，执行速度比先前轻量流匹配策略提升3.2倍。

Conclusion: 空间调优和生成式隐空间流控制显著提升轻量VLA性能，为高效、空间感知的机器人操控提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have shown strong multimodal understanding and spatial grounding, but their computational cost limits real-time robotic control. In contrast, lightweight models are suitable for edge deployment but often suffer from "spatial blindness", namely weak native spatial prediction ability. Training Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models on mixed human demonstrations can also degrade policy performance due to highly diverse behaviors. To address these limitations, we propose XS-VLA, a two-stage framework for efficient and spatially grounded robotic manipulation. First, we distill spatial semantic knowledge from Qwen3-VL-4B into the SmolVLM2-0.25B backbone by fine-tuning on curated coarse-grained spatial descriptions, turning the lightweight model into a spatially grounded engine. Second, we use this enhanced backbone to condition a Latent Flow Matching policy. Unlike deterministic controllers, our policy combines a Conditional Variational Autoencoder (CVAE) with Flow Matching dynamics to model complex multimodal action distributions. On the LIBERO benchmark, XS-VLA achieves state-of-the-art performance among models with fewer than 0.5B parameters. It improves average success rates by up to 7.2 percent, including a 23 percent gain on LIBERO-Long, over the SmolVLA 0.25B baseline, and outperforms the larger 2.2B vanilla SmolVLA. Ablations show that spatial tuning and generative latent flow control substantially improve lightweight VLA performance, delivering a 3.2 times speedup in mission execution over the previous lightweight flow matching policy.

</details>


### [375] [SoftVTBench: A Safety-Aware Visuo-Tactile Benchmark for Physically Constrained Robotic Manipulation of Deformable Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04234)
*Bowen Jing,Mingxin Wang,Ruiyang Hao,Chenchen Ge,Hanwen Shen,Junjie He,Yang Cui,Yiming Hou,Weitao Zhou,Jiawei Wang,Minglei Li,Dandan Zhang,Ding Zhao,Houde Liu,Xiaofan Li,Si Liu,Ping Luo,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SoftVTBench是一个面向可变形物体操作的安全性感知视觉-触觉基准，在Isaac Sim中构建，通过有限元仿真模拟可变形物体，分别报告任务成功率和安全成功率（无掉落、变形低于阈值），实验表明仅考虑任务成功率会高估策略性能，而触觉感知能显著提升安全成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作基准主要关注任务是否完成，忽略了操作过程中的物理安全性（如物体滑落、过度变形），缺乏一个能够同时评估任务成功和物理安全约束的基准。

Method: 在Isaac Sim中构建基于有限元仿真的可变形物体操作环境，提供多视角RGB、RGB触觉传感（含标记点运动）、本体感知和语言指令输入，定义4组任务（物体类型×变化轴），分别报告目标成功率和安全成功率（无掉落且峰值变形低于标定阈值），并实现pi0.5基线。

Result: 仅考虑成功率的评估会严重高估策略表现；引入触觉感知将安全成功率从21.4%提升至35.6%（物体中心可变形任务），同时保持相近的任务成功率。

Conclusion: SoftVTBench为研究物理交互约束下的视觉-触觉可变形物体操作提供了可复现的基准，并证明了触觉感知对提升操作安全性的关键作用。

Abstract: Deformable object manipulation poses challenges beyond task completion: successful execution must also maintain safe physical interaction, holding the object stably without slip or drop while avoiding excessive deformation. However, existing manipulation benchmarks are predominantly success-oriented and rarely evaluate whether a policy remains physically safe throughout execution. We present SoftVTBench, a safety-aware visuo-tactile benchmark for physically constrained deformable object manipulation. Built in Isaac Sim with finite-element-simulated deformable objects, SoftVTBench provides multi-view RGB observations, RGB tactile sensing with marker motion, proprioception, and language instructions, and defines four matched task suites over object type (deformable vs. rigid) and variation axis (object vs. spatial). It separately reports Goal Success and Safety Success; the latter additionally requires no drop and peak deformation below a calibrated object-specific threshold, measured from policy-hidden privileged Finite Element Method (FEM) states. We implement pi0.5-based baselines under this protocol. Experiments show that success-only evaluation substantially overstates policy performance, as a large fraction of goal-completing rollouts still violate physical safety. Furthermore, incorporating tactile sensing improves Safety Success (e.g., from 21.4% to 35.6% on object-centric deformable tasks) and reduces object deformation during execution, while maintaining comparable Goal Success. SoftVTBench provides a reproducible benchmark for studying visuo-tactile deformable manipulation under physical interaction constraints.

</details>


### [376] [Integrated Graph Search and Model Predictive Control for Smooth and Efficient Path Planning in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04259)
*Duc-Tien Bui,Ngoc Thinh Nguyen,Hung Duy Nguyen,Dong Bi,Tomislav Mihalj,Arno Eichberger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种序列化路径规划框架，先通过Dijkstra图搜索生成粗略路径，再构建空间变化的凸侧向安全走廊，将避障约束转化为连续可行性约束，最后在走廊内用MPC优化路径平滑性和动态可行性。在直道和弯道多超车场景中，计算成本降低约28-30%，且横向加速度、曲率和急动度指标更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中路径规划需要在安全性、舒适性、动态可行性和计算效率之间取得平衡，现有方法难以同时满足这些要求，尤其是图搜索的离散性与优化方法的连续性之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 1) 用Dijkstra在离散栅格上搜索粗略路径；2) 利用粗略路径构造空间变化的凸侧向安全走廊，将离散避障转化为连续约束；3) 在走廊内构建MPC问题，通过惩罚横向偏移的三阶空间导数（急动度）来优化路径平滑性。

Result: 在CarMaker高保真仿真中，与多项式拟合+二次规划方法相比，横向加速度、曲率、急动度均更低；直道计算成本降低28.08%，弯道降低29.52%。

Conclusion: 图搜索结构与MPC公式的结合能在结构化驾驶环境中有效平衡路径平滑性和计算效率。

Abstract: Path planning is a fundamental component of autonomous vehicles, where achieving safe, comfortable, and dynamically feasible paths while ensuring computational efficiency remains a significant challenge. This paper presents a sequential path planning framework in which a rough path obtained from graph search is explicitly exploited to guide a Model Predictive Control (MPC)-based path refinement. A rough path is first obtained via Dijkstra search on a discretized grid and is then used to construct a spatially varying convex lateral safety corridor that explicitly captures obstacle avoidance constraints, transforming discrete obstacle avoidance decisions into continuous feasibility constraints for optimization. Within this corridor, an MPC problem is formulated to refine the path, enabling efficient optimization while maintaining path smoothness by penalizing the third-order spatial derivative of the lateral offset over a prediction horizon. The proposed algorithm is evaluated in multiple overtaking scenarios on both straight and curved roads, including cases with single and multiple target vehicles, using high-fidelity environment simulations (i.e., CarMaker). Compared with the previous study, which used polynomial fitting and a quadratic programming method, the proposed approach consistently achieves lower lateral acceleration, curvature, and jerk while reducing computational cost by 28.08% on straight roads and 29.52% on curved roads. These results demonstrate that exploiting graph-search structure within an MPC formulation provides an effective balance between path smoothness and computational efficiency for autonomous vehicles in structured driving environments.

</details>


### [377] [FLOAT Drone for Physical Interaction: Lateral Airflow Reduction, Wrench Modeling, and Adaptive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04260)
*Junxiao Lin,Kehan Zhou,Shuhang Ji,Yimin Peng,Shen Wang,Jialiang Hou,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FLOAT Drone，一种带有伺服驱动控制面的共轴双旋翼全驱动无人机，可在近距离物理交互中产生六自由度力/力矩，同时减少对目标侧向气流干扰。通过力匹配CFD对比验证了其相比倾斜旋翼方案的气流优势，并采用高保真多项式气动力矩模型与非线性约束分配器实现实时力控。实验证明其在抽屉推拉（2cm间隙）、地面效应和负载扰动下的控制精度优于线性基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机空中物理交互需要能够施加接触力同时保持稳定飞行的平台，现有方案在近距离任务中难以同时满足多维力生成、紧凑性和减少对目标侧向气流三个设计需求。

Method: 采用共轴双旋翼布局提供紧凑推进，在旋翼下洗流中嵌入伺服驱动控制面生成侧向力和力矩，实现六自由度力生成；通过力匹配CFD与倾斜旋翼方案对比；从精密力测量中辨识高保真多项式气动力矩模型，嵌入约束非线性分配器实现实时力跟踪。

Result: 在对比飞行和交互实验中，所提框架在线性分配基线上提升控制精度，能够抑制地面效应和负载扰动，并成功实现2cm间隙的抽屉推拉操作。

Conclusion: FLOAT Drone通过共轴双旋翼加控制面的设计有效解决了近距离空中物理交互的三个关键需求，非线性分配器显著提升了力控精度，为无人机在受限空间中的接触式操作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Aerial physical interaction represents a promising direction for next-generation unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), but it requires an aerial platform that can exert contact forces while maintaining stable flight. For close-proximity tasks, this translates into three coupled design requirements: multidimensional wrench generation for stable contact, compactness for maneuverability and safety in confined spaces, and reduced lateral airflow toward the target when generating horizontal force. This article presents FLOAT Drone, a fully actuated coaxial UAV with servo-driven control surfaces for close-proximity physical interaction. The coaxial dual-rotor layout provides a compact propulsion layout, while the control surfaces, immersed in the rotor downwash, generate lateral forces and moments for 6-DoF wrench generation. A force-matched computational fluid dynamics (CFD) comparison with a tilted-rotor alternative quantifies the reduction in target-facing lateral airflow. To account for nonlinear rotor--control-surface coupling in the rotor wake, a high-fidelity polynomial aerodynamic wrench model is identified from precision force measurements and embedded in a constrained nonlinear allocator for real-time wrench tracking. Comparative flight and interaction experiments show that the proposed framework improves control accuracy over linear allocation baselines, rejects ground-effect and payload disturbances, and enables close-proximity drawer push--pull manipulation through a $2~\mathrm{cm}$ handle clearance.

</details>


### [378] [HALO-WA: Hybrid-Attention Latent-Guided Online Reinforcement Learning for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04265)
*Angen Ye,Weijie Ke,Xiaofeng Wang,Xinze Chen,Chaojun Ni,Guosheng Zhao,Boyuan Wang,Zheng Zhu,Junjie Xie,Dapeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HALO-WA通过混合注意力潜变量引导的在线强化学习适配器，对世界-动作（WA）模型生成的长时间动作片段进行精调，解决精密操作任务中最后几毫米的误差问题，将平均成功率从26.4%提升至87.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WA模型生成的长程动作片段在真实世界的精密操作中易受标定、感知和接触动力学误差影响，尤其在最后几毫米的对准或插入阶段经常失败，缺乏快速在线适应部署误差的能力。

Method: 提出HALO-WA框架，在预训练WA模型基础上附加轻量级演员-评论家适配器，通过混合注意力结构同时保持动作片段的时间一致性并从WA潜变量中读取任务相关信息，利用在线强化学习进行快速微调以适应真实部署误差。

Result: 在4个真实精密操作任务上，平均成功率从WA-base的26.4%提升到87.1%，超越最强基线19.2个百分点，仅需45-75分钟在线训练。在RoboTwin仿真中也有补充实验验证。

Conclusion: HALO-WA证明了在预训练WA模型上叠加轻量在线RL适配器是解决精密操作部署误差问题的有效路径，兼顾了动作片段的长期一致性和细粒度修正能力。

Abstract: World-action (WA) models can generate long-horizon action chunks for general-purpose robotic manipulation, but they remain vulnerable to calibration, perception, and contact-dynamics errors in real-world precision tasks, often failing in the final few millimeters of alignment or insertion. We propose HALO-WA, a hybrid-attention latent-guided online reinforcement learning (RL) framework for WA models, which leverages latent features and action priors from the WA generation process through a lightweight actor-critic adapter to enable fast online adaptation to real deployment errors. HALO-WA introduces a hybrid-attention structure that preserves the temporal consistency of action chunks while reading task-relevant information from WA latents conditioned on visual context and end-stage correction requirements, thereby producing refined action chunks. We validate HALO-WA on four real-world precision manipulation tasks, where it improves the average success rate from 26.4\% for WA-base to 87.1\%, outperforming the strongest baseline by 19.2 percentage points while requiring only 45--75 minutes of online training per task. To facilitate reproducibility, we further conduct supplementary simulation experiments in RoboTwin and release the code at https://github.com/YeanRoot/HALO-WA.

</details>


### [379] [GPU-Accelerated Polygonal Signed Distance Functions for Real-Time Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04310)
*Taekwon Ga,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种多边形符号距离函数(PSDF)，用于凸多边形机器人足迹与障碍物之间的几何精确碰撞检测，并将其嵌入基于序贯二次规划的模型预测控制(PSDF-MPC)，通过GPU批处理加速约束评估，实现了高密度多边形环境中的实时避障控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在障碍密集环境中，优化-based局部规划与控制需要高频碰撞避让约束评估，但其计算负载常成为控制周期瓶颈。现有方法难以兼顾计算效率与几何保真度，因此需要一种既高效又几何精确的碰撞约束表达方式。

Method: 提出PSDF：对凸多边形机器人足迹与障碍物边界边之间的几何精确符号距离函数，实现为无权重、无分支的张量化几何管线，支持批量GPU执行与自动微分。将PSDF嵌入MPC，通过序贯二次规划实时迭代方案局部线性化阶段安全约束，解耦CPU/GPU计算——GPU评估批处理PSDF值与梯度，CPU求解稀疏二次规划（维度由系统维度与horizon长度决定，与障碍物特征无关）。

Result: 微基准测试显示PSDF在符号距离查询上优于基线方法。闭环仿真与真实世界导航实验（包括与优化基线对比）表明，PSDF-MPC在密集多边形环境中保持了实时可行性与鲁棒碰撞避让能力。

Conclusion: PSDF-MPC通过GPU加速的几何精确碰撞约束评估，有效解决了障碍密集环境中的实时避障计算瓶颈，实现了高效且鲁棒的导航控制。

Abstract: Optimization-based local planning and control require high-rate collision-avoidance constraint evaluation over a prediction horizon. In obstacle-dense environments, where feasible space is limited and the constraints become increasingly complex, the computational workload often dominates the control-cycle runtime. The resulting bottleneck motivates collision-avoidance constraints that combine computational efficiency with geometric fidelity. The proposed Polygonal Signed Distance Function (PSDF) is a geometry-exact signed distance function between a convex polygonal robot footprint and obstacles represented by their boundary edges. It is implemented as a weight-free, branch-free tensorized geometric pipeline enabling batched GPU execution and automatic differentiation. The PSDF is embedded into model predictive control by locally linearizing the stage-wise safety constraints within a sequential quadratic programming-based real-time iteration scheme, yielding the PSDF-embedded model predictive controller (PSDF-MPC). The design separates CPU/GPU computation so that the GPU evaluates batched PSDF values and gradients while the CPU solves a sparse quadratic program whose dimension is determined by system dimensions and horizon length, not by obstacle features. Microbenchmarks show that PSDF scales favorably against signed-distance query baselines. Closed-loop simulated and real-world navigation experiments, including comparisons with optimization-based baselines, demonstrate that PSDF-MPC maintains real-time feasibility and robust collision avoidance in dense polygonal environments.

</details>


### [380] [Agent-driven Long-tail Simulation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04331)
*Junru Gu,Lijin Yang,Jianing Huang,Shu Liu,Zhongzhan Huang,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于大语言模型指令驱动的自动驾驶仿真框架，周围交通参与者由LLM通过结构化动作接口控制，实现有意向且反应式的行为；同时引入SemanticPlan基准，在长尾语义丰富的闭环规划场景中评估现有规划器，发现其仍难以在这些场景下安全高效完成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶闭环仿真器依赖日志回放或基于规则的智能体，行为多样性有限且难以覆盖长尾场景，需要更真实、更具交互性的仿真环境来评估和提升自动驾驶规划系统。

Method: 提出智能体驱动的仿真框架：使用指令遵循的大语言模型（LLM）通过结构化动作接口控制周围道路参与者，兼顾有意行为与物理合理性；在此基础上构建SemanticPlan基准，在nuPlan真实场景基础上加入多个遵循多样化语言指令的交互式智能体，生成闭环规划的长尾语义场景。

Result: 在当前最先进的规划器上进行评估，结果表明它们在长尾语义场景下始终难以实现安全且有效的任务完成，说明这些场景仍具有挑战性。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的交互式仿真能生成更具多样性和挑战性的长尾场景，为评估自动驾驶规划器提供了更有力的测试平台，但现有规划器在此类场景下表现不佳，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Evaluating autonomous driving systems in closed-loop settings requires realistic and interactive simulation, yet existing simulators largely rely on log replay or rule-based agents, limiting behavioral diversity and long-tail coverage. We propose an agent-driven simulation framework in which surrounding road participants are controlled by instruction-following large language models through a structured action interface, enabling intentional and reactive behaviors while preserving physical plausibility. Furthermore, we introduce SemanticPlan, a benchmark of closed-loop planning in long-tail and semantically rich scenarios that augment real nuPlan scenes with multiple interactive agents following diverse language instructions. Evaluation results show that state-of-the-art planners still struggle to consistently achieve safe and effective task completion, suggesting that these long-tail scenarios remain challenging.

</details>


### [381] [A Perception-Manipulation Robotics System for Food Cutting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04367)
*Xinyuan Luo,Wenzhen Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向烹饪机器人切割任务的感知-操作框架，包含基于力的刀具选择模块和基于强化学习的自适应切割策略，在未知食材上刀具选择成功率达100%，性能与人类操作者相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 烹饪机器人中切割任务至关重要，但食材性质多样，需要不同的切割策略和刀具才能最优处理，现有方法难以自适应选择刀具和切割策略。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 预切阶段采集力数据，通过刀具选择模块挑选合适刀具；2) 自适应切割阶段使用强化学习平衡切割速度与能量效率。

Result: 刀具选择模块在未知食材上成功率达100%；RL策略在性能上优于固定策略，并与人类操作者结果相当。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效解决食材多样性下的自适应切割问题，实现了接近人类水平的切割性能。

Abstract: In the development of cooking robots, mastering the task of cutting is crucial. A significant challenge lies in the diverse properties of food, which necessitate distinct cutting policies and even different knives for optimal processing. This paper presents a perception-manipulation framework for food-cutting tasks. Our system features a knife selection module that utilizes force data from a preliminary fixed trial cut to select the appropriate knife for the given food. This is followed by an adaptive cutting phase using reinforcement learning (RL) to balance cutting speed and energy efficiency. In our experiments, the knife selection module achieved 100% successful rate on unseen food, and we compared the performances of fixed policy, RL policy, with human operators. Our method not only achieves high performance but also demonstrates comparable results to those of human participants.

</details>


### [382] [SurgAM: Surgical Affordance Map Prediction with Multimodal Feature Fusion for Robot Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04378)
*Lei Song,Yonghao Long,Mengya Xu,Jiayi Geng,Xiuyuan Chen,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出手术可操作区域预测（surgical affordance prediction）任务，通过自适应特征融合框架（自监督ViT+生成模型编码器）、层次化提示学习和场景引导注意力解码器，从视觉数据中识别可操作区域，并在肺部和前列腺模型上验证了下游自动化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术自动化中视觉场景理解与自主动作规划之间存在鸿沟，现有工作集中于场景感知（工具识别、分割），但缺乏对可操作区域（affordance）的预测和理解。

Method: 提出自适应特征融合框架，结合自监督ViT（语义理解）和生成模型编码器（空间感知）；层次化提示学习适应流程上下文；场景引导注意力解码器聚焦手术关键区域、抑制背景干扰；构建包含吸引、夹闭、牵拉三种动作的affordance标注数据集。

Result: 在构建的数据集上达到SOTA性能；在真实肺部和前列腺模型上下游自动化任务中，预测的affordance地图成功实现了自主手术动作。

Conclusion: 手术affordance预测是连接视觉理解与动作规划的有效桥梁，所提框架具有较好的泛化和实用能力。

Abstract: Surgical automation is being increasingly studied, yet bridging visual scene understanding with autonomous action planning remains a fundamental challenge. While much research effort has been made on scene perception (e.g., tool recognition and scene segmentation), understanding and predicting actionable possibilities for surgical automation is still underexplored. In this paper, we introduce surgical affordance prediction, which identifies actionable regions for fundamental surgical actions from visual data. Specifically, a novel adaptive feature fusion framework is proposed that leverages the complementary strengths of a self-supervised vision transformer encoder for its superior semantic understanding and a large-scale generative model encoder for its spatially-aware capability. Furthermore, we introduce a hierarchical prompt learning mechanism to adapt to varying procedural contexts. Finally, a scene-guided attention decoder is proposed to focus on critical surgical areas while suppressing background distractions. To validate the effectiveness, we established a new dataset, derived from publicly available surgical datasets with affordance annotations for three basic surgical actions: aspiration, clipping, and retraction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance. Moreover, we validate our framework's applicability for downstream automation on a realistic lung and prostate phantom, and results show that the predicted affordance maps successfully enable autonomous surgical actions.

</details>


### [383] [ACE-Brain-0.5: A Unified Embodied Foundational Model for Physical Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04426)
*ACE-Brain Team,:,Ziyang Gong,Haoming Gu,Zehang Luo,Tianyi Zhang,Tao Tao,Yixiao Chi,Zhe Liu,Lingsi Zhu,Jingyuan Liu,Anke Tang,Songze Li,Yilun Kong,Ningjing Liu,Tianyu Zhu,Yunpeng Qing,Shuang Luo,Xiang Liu,Shi Fu,Dawei Nie,Sixiang Liu,Zhexi Wen,Feng Pan,Xiaofeng Wang,Zhi Hou,Chunxiao Liu,Xue Yang,Junchi Yan,Hengshuang Zhao,Dacheng Tao,Xiaogang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ACE-Brain-0.5是一个统一的基础模型，将具身智能组织为五个耦合功能（空间感知、决策、交互、自监控、自改进），基于8B骨干网络通过SSR+方法实现任务向量融合，并在15个基准测试中超越前代模型，朝向通用物理具身AI迈出一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身AI系统碎片化严重：端到端策略缺乏空间推理和执行评估，机器人智能体系统缺乏共享表征学习，阻碍了通用物理具身AI的发展。

Method: 提出ACE-Brain-0.5，在ACE-Brain-0空间智能基础上，通过SSR+（Scaffold-Specialize-Reconcile + Reactivate）方法将空间感知、决策、交互、自监控四个功能整合进单一8B骨干；第五功能自改进通过外部框架从rollouts更新任务模式、空间记忆和失败恢复案例实现。

Result: 在15个基准测试上，18个空间感知与定位基准中14个超越ACE-Brain-0；导航和操作任务达到竞争性能；在ID和OOD场景下提供强进度估计能力。

Conclusion: ACE-Brain-0.5展示了将碎片化具身功能统一为闭环基础模型的可行性，是迈向通用物理具身AI的早期但重要的一步。

Abstract: Embodied AI is moving from isolated perception or action modules toward physical agents that understand, plan under goals, act through robot bodies, monitor progress, and improve from experience. Existing systems address this loop only in parts: end-to-end policies generate actions but often lack spatial reasoning, planning, and execution assessment, while robot-agent systems orchestrate tools or specialists but do not learn a shared representation. This fragmentation limits general Physical Agentic AI. We present ACE-Brain-0.5, a unified embodied foundation model that organizes robot intelligence into five coupled functions: spatial perception, decision making, embodied interaction, self-monitoring, and self-improvement. Built on ACE-Brain-0, which established spatial intelligence as a shared scaffold across robot platforms, ACE-Brain-0.5 extends an understanding-centric model into a closed-loop foundation model. A single 8B backbone instantiates the first four functions: grounding objects and affordances, reasoning over 3D and egocentric spatial relations, decomposing instructions into subgoals, generating navigation and manipulation actions, and estimating progress for verification and recovery. To unify these capabilities without cross-task interference, we introduce SSR+, which extends Scaffold-Specialize-Reconcile with a Reactivate stage after task-vector merging. The fifth function, self-improvement, is realized by a companion framework that updates external execution state, including task schemas, spatial memory, and failure-recovery cases, from rollouts. Across fifteen benchmarks, ACE-Brain-0.5 improves over ACE-Brain-0 on 14 of 18 spatial perception and grounding benchmarks, achieves competitive navigation and manipulation performance, and provides strong progress estimation in ID and OOD settings. Together, these results mark an early step toward general Physical Agentic AI.

</details>


### [384] [RoboDojo: A Unified Sim-and-Real Benchmark for Comprehensive Evaluation of Generalist Robot Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04434)
*Tianxing Chen,Yue Chen,Zixuan Li,Junyuan Tang,Kailun Su,Weijie Wan,Baijun Chen,Haoran Lu,Haowen Yan,Honghao Su,Zhiyang Dou,Kaixuan Wang,Dandan Zhang,Yunze Liu,Yan Qin,Qiwei Liang,Qiwei Wu,Zijian Lin,Wenwei Lin,Yuran Wang,Minghua He,Tianshu Wu,Ruihai Wu,Jingquan Zhou,Kai-Chong Lei,Haibao Yu,Yuanfeng Ji,Weiyang Jin,Guanyu Lin,Xiaofan Li,Qi Xiong,Renjing Xu,Zhongyu Li,Wenhao Chai,Enze Xie,Ziwei Wang,Yao Mu,Hao Dong,Wojciech Matusik,Mingyu Ding,Wenbo Ding,Ping Luo,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboDojo，一个统一仿真与真实世界的通用机器人操作策略综合评估基准，包含42项仿真任务和18项真实任务，覆盖五大能力维度，并集成30种策略进行系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略评估基准存在三大局限：任务简单、技能覆盖窄、能力维度有限；仿真与真实评估割裂（仿真缺少物理部署挑战，真实评估成本高且难以复现）；缺乏可扩展、可复现的统一评估体系。

Method: 构建RoboDojo基准：1）42项仿真任务评估5个维度（泛化、记忆、精度、长时程、开放指令跟随）；2）18项真实任务评估物理部署挑战；3）支持异构并行仿真（Isaac Sim）实现可扩展评估；4）RoboDojo-RealEval提供云访问、标准化硬件、场景重置、评估协议、部署接口的复现系统；5）XPolicyLab统一策略集成框架，30种策略无需大幅适配即可在仿真和真实环境评估。

Result: 集成30种策略并在RoboDojo上完成评估，建立公开排行榜，系统性分析了当前策略的性能表现。

Conclusion: RoboDojo提供了一个统一的仿真-真实评估平台，填补了通用机器人操作策略系统性评估的空白，通过标准化评估协议和可复现系统，有助于推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: Generalist robot manipulation policies have advanced rapidly, yet existing benchmarks remain limited in systematically evaluating their capabilities. Many rely on simple, short-horizon, or skill-narrow tasks with limited capability coverage, and are often conducted only in simulation or only in the real world. Simulation enables scalable feedback but misses physical deployment challenges, while real-world evaluation is costly, time-consuming, and difficult to reproduce. We introduce RoboDojo, a unified sim-and-real benchmark for comprehensive evaluation of generalist robot manipulation policies. RoboDojo includes 42 simulation tasks and 18 real-world tasks covering diverse and complementary manipulation capabilities. The simulation benchmark evaluates five dimensions: generalization, memory, precision, long-horizon execution, and open-vocabulary instruction following, while the real-world benchmark exposes policies to challenging physical-world deployment conditions. RoboDojo supports scalable evaluation through heterogeneous parallel simulation in Isaac Sim and provides RoboDojo-RealEval, a reproducible real-world evaluation system with remote cloud access, standardized hardware, scene reset, evaluation protocol, and deployment interface. Together with XPolicyLab, policies can be integrated once and evaluated across simulation and real-world settings with minimal adaptation. We integrate 30 policies into XPolicyLab and evaluate them on RoboDojo, establishing a public leaderboard and systematic analysis of current policy performance. The website is available at http://robodojo-benchmark.com/.

</details>


### [385] [Robustness Verification of an Autonomous Underwater Vehicle-based Plankton Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04453)
*Abdelrahman Sayed Sayed,Pierre-Jean Meyer,Asgeir J. Sørensen,Mohamed Ghazel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于可达性分析的鲁棒性验证框架，用于原位浮游生物分类器，并引入神经常微分方程（Neural ODE）分类模型，以应对动态水下环境中噪声和伪影导致的误分类问题，提高自主采样的可靠性并减少人工验证工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前搭载AI的水下自主机器人（AUV）原位成像分类方法在动态非结构化环境中鲁棒性差，环境噪声和非生物伪影导致CNN分类器频繁误分类，需要海洋生物学家耗时的人工验证，亟需形式化的鲁棒性验证方法。

Method: 1）提出基于可达性分析（reachability analysis）的鲁棒性验证框架，对分类器进行形式化验证；2）引入连续时间神经常微分方程（Neural ODE）作为分类模型，结合SilCam粒子成像仪的高分辨率成像能力；3）将验证框架作为自动化过滤器，提供模型对模糊/扰动数据稳定性的形式化保证。

Result: 通过实验展示了所提框架能够形式化验证Neural ODE模型对环境扰动的鲁棒性，作为自动化过滤机制有效提升自主采样的可靠性并减少后处理工作量。

Conclusion: 基于可达性分析的鲁棒性验证框架结合Neural ODE分类模型，能够为水下原位浮游生物分类提供形式化的稳定性保证，降低了手动验证负担，提升了AUV自主采样的可靠性。

Abstract: The assessment of planktonic standing stocks and microorganism structures is critical for understanding upper ocean biological processes. Currently, autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) equipped with in-situ optical imaging and artificial intelligence (AI) methods offer a promising solution for persistent surveillance, mapping and monitoring of planktonic life. However, current AI methods often lack robustness in dynamic, unstructured environments, where environmental noise and non-biological artifacts lead to frequent misclassifications. Standard convolutional neural network (CNN) classifiers often struggle with such conditions, leading to misclassifications that require time-consuming manual validation by marine biologists.
  To address this issue, we propose a novel robustness verification framework for in-situ plankton classifiers based on reachability analysis. We also introduce a continuous-time neural ordinary differential equation (neural ODE) classification model leveraging the high-resolution imaging capabilities of the SilCam particle imager. In this paper, we demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework by formally verifying the robustness of the neural ODE model against environmental perturbations. We demonstrate that our verification framework acts as an automated filter providing formal guarantees of model stability against ambiguous data, thereby improving the reliability of autonomous sampling and reducing the post-processing workload.

</details>


### [386] [Mask2Real-WM: Segmentation Masks as a Sim-to-Real Bridge for Controllable Dexterous World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04546)
*Riccardo O. Feingold,Davide Liconti,Chenyu Yang,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mask2Real-WM 提出两阶段动作条件世界模型：先预测分割掩码序列（动力学模型），再将掩码渲染为逼真RGB（ControlNet+Stable Video Diffusion）。动力学模型在合成数据（50h+）上预训练，再微调于少量真实数据（<2.5h），实现灵巧手23-DoF的精细动作控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人灵巧操作的像素级预测中，真实数据稀缺且sim-to-real gap大。现有单阶段模型难以捕捉每自由度（per-DoF）的细粒度动作效果。

Method: 将像素预测解耦为：1）动力学模型预测未来分割掩码（从过去掩码+23-DoF动作），在分割空间利用小sim-to-real gap进行大规模合成数据预训练+真实数据微调；2）渲染模型用ControlNet增强的Stable Video Diffusion将分割掩码转为逼真RGB图像。

Result: 在灵巧抓取基准上，掩码条件+模拟预训练两者缺一不可，才能实现所有23个自由度的逐关节动作可控性。单阶段基线只能捕获粗略末端轨迹，无法反映细粒度关节效果。

Conclusion: 解耦预测+分割空间预训练有效克服了真实数据稀疏和sim-to-real gap问题，实现了高自由度灵巧操作的细粒度动作条件世界模型。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models allow robots to predict the future consequences of candidate actions without additional physical interaction, supporting policy evaluation, planning, and data augmentation. We present Mask2Real-WM, a two-stage action-conditioned world model for dexterous manipulation that decouples pixel prediction into a dynamics model and a rendering model. The dynamics model predicts future segmentation masks from past masks and 23-DoF action sequences. The rendering model maps the predicted masks to photorealistic RGB using a ControlNet-augmented Stable Video Diffusion backbone. The smaller sim-to-real gap in segmentation space enables the dynamics model to benefit from large-scale pretraining on over 50 h of synthetic simulation data, followed by fine-tuning on fewer than 2.5 h of real demonstrations. Experiments on a dexterous pick-and-place benchmark show that mask conditioning and simulation pretraining are both required for per-DoF action controllability across all 23 degrees of freedom. In contrast, monolithic baselines capture broad hand and end-effector trajectories but do not reliably reflect fine-grained, per-joint action effects.

</details>


### [387] [HUGS: Guiding Unified Dexterous Grasp Synthesis Across Modes and Scales via Learned Human Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04554)
*Mingrui Yu,Yongpeng Jiang,Yongyi Jia,Kangchen Lv,Li Huang,Yi Ren,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HUGS提出基于人类抓取先验的统一灵巧抓取合成框架，通过学习物体条件化的人类抓取偏好引导力闭合优化，在2cm-30cm尺度范围内统一从两指到双臂的抓取模式合成，产出320万抓取数据并实现真实世界部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧抓取合成方法依赖人工设计期望接触和初始化启发式策略，难以在合成成功率和多样性之间取得平衡，且无法统一处理从两指捏取到双臂抓取的多尺度多模式抓取问题。

Method: 构建包含304个物体、1800个抓取的紧凑人类抓取数据集，训练物体条件化人类抓取先验；先验引导下游力闭合感知优化，自适应提议接触模式和手腕初始化，无需直接重定向人类演示。

Result: 合成320万机器人抓取（157K场景），覆盖2cm-30cm物体半对角线长度及两指到双臂多种模式；训练模型在真实世界自主选择接触模式，实现从螺丝到大箱子的抓取。

Conclusion: 人类先验引导框架有效统一了多尺度多模式灵巧抓取合成，在合成覆盖率和成功率间取得更好平衡，生成数据可驱动真实世界机器人抓取。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping across diverse object scales requires contact modes ranging from two-finger pinches to bimanual grasps. Existing dexterous grasp synthesis methods reduce the high-dimensional optimization space with manually designed expected contacts and initialization heuristics, which struggle to balance synthesis success rate and diversity. We present HUGS (Human-prior-guided Unified Dexterous Grasp Synthesis), a human-prior-guided framework for unified dexterous grasp synthesis across modes and scales. Instead of directly retargeting human demonstrations, HUGS learns an object-conditioned human prior that captures human grasp preferences and guides downstream force-closure-aware optimization. The prior is trained on a compact self-collected human grasp dataset with 1.8K grasps over 304 objects, providing broad coverage of object scales and contact modes. During synthesis, HUGS adaptively proposes contact modes and wrist initializations, substantially improving the balance between contact-mode coverage and synthesis success rate over heuristic-based methods. With HUGS, we synthesize 3.2M robotic grasps over 157K scenes, spanning object half-diagonal lengths from 2 cm to 30 cm and modes from two-finger to bimanual grasps. Models trained on the synthesized dataset autonomously select appropriate contact modes in the real world, enabling grasping from screws to large boxes.

</details>


### [388] [Simple-to-Complex Structured Demonstrations for Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04591)
*Xinchuan Qiu,Yi Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型中演示数据组织和采集策略被忽视的问题，提出了一种由简到繁的结构化演示采集策略，包含子技能分解、环境标准化和任务复杂度递进三个原则，在积木抓取排序和毛巾折叠任务上提升了成功率和训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA研究集中于模型架构、训练策略和数据集规模，忽略了演示数据的组织和采集方式对模仿学习效率、训练稳定性和策略泛化的直接影响。

Method: 提出三种通用原则：(i) 将复杂操作任务分解为逐步可学的子技能；(ii) 标准化交互环境以减少不必要变异；(iii) 按递增的任务复杂度组织演示数据。让VLA模型先习得基础操作技能，再学习复杂任务组合。

Result: 在积木抓取排序和毛巾折叠任务上，相比直接采集端到端完整轨迹的基线方法，在任务成功率和训练稳定性上取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 演示数据组织是VLA学习中此前未被充分探索但重要的因素，为高效技能获取、可扩展数据集构建和长时域操作提供了实用见解。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation by integrating visual perception, language understanding, and robot action generation. Existing research has primarily focused on improving model architectures, training strategies, and dataset scale, while little attention has been paid to how demonstrations are collected and organized. We identify demonstration organization as a fundamental yet overlooked aspect of imitation learning, as it directly affects policy learning efficiency, training stability, and policy generalization. To address this gap, we propose a simple-to-complex structured demonstration collection strategy for VLA learning using a dual-arm robotic platform. Our approach systematically organizes data through three general principles: (i) decomposing complex manipulation tasks into progressively learnable sub-skills, (ii) standardizing the interaction environment to reduce unnecessary variability, and (iii) organizing demonstrations according to progressively increasing task complexity. This structured design enables VLA models to first acquire fundamental manipulation skills before learning increasingly complex task compositions, facilitating more effective learning of long-horizon manipulation tasks. We evaluate the proposed strategy on two representative robotic manipulation tasks: block grasping and sorting, and towel folding. Experimental results show consistent improvements in task success rate and training stability compared with the baseline method of directly collecting end-to-end complete task trajectories. These findings highlight demonstration organization as a previously underexplored but important factor in VLA learning and provide practical insights into efficient skill acquisition, scalable dataset construction, and long-horizon robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [389] [SEAM: Smooth Execution of Action-Chunked Motion for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04609)
*Dijia Zhan,Xuemiao Xu,Jinyi Li,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SEAM方法，一种无需训练的推理时方法，通过在流匹配VLA策略中利用前一chunk未执行尾段作为一致性参考，使用速度引导损失引导（VLS）在每一步Euler后施加闭式校正，消除多模态分叉导致的chunk间不连续，在LIBERO-10上降低边界jerk 28%、过渡不连续27%，保持任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略执行固定长度动作chunk时存在多模态分叉问题：相邻chunk从独立高斯潜变量生成，可能收敛到不兼容的轨迹模态，导致chunk边界处出现突变不连续。现有方法需反向传播、拒绝采样或重新训练，代价高。

Method: SEAM：训练无关的推理时方法。核心机制Velocity-guided Loss Steering (VLS)，利用前一chunk未执行尾段作为解析一致性参考，推导时间依赖目标，在每个Euler步后施加闭式校正，无需通过策略网络反向传播。

Result: 在LIBERO-10上使用pi_0.5，边界jerk降低28%，chunk过渡不连续降低27%，保持基线任务成功率，去噪循环成本接近无引导基线。

Conclusion: SEAM有效解决了VLA中动作chunk间的不连续问题，是一种轻量、高效、无需重新训练的推理时平滑方法。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies that execute fixed-length action chunks can exhibit multimodal bifurcation: a cross-chunk inconsistency in which adjacent chunks generated from independent Gaussian latents can converge to incompatible trajectory modes, producing abrupt discontinuities at chunk boundaries. Existing remedies either require backpropagation through the policy at each denoising step, rely on rejection sampling, or require retraining, each trading computational cost or task reliability for smoother transitions. We propose SEAM (Smooth Execution of Action-Chunked Motion), a training-free inference-time method for flow matching VLAs. SEAM exploits a simple synchronous-execution insight: after the robot consumes the executed prefix, the previous chunk's unexecuted tail is already available as an analytic consistency reference. Its core mechanism, Velocity-guided Loss Steering (VLS), derives a time-dependent target from this tail and applies a closed-form correction after each Euler step without backpropagating through the policy network. On LIBERO-10 with pi_0.5, SEAM reduces boundary jerk by 28%, reduces chunk transition discontinuity by 27%, preserves baseline-level task success, and keeps denoising-loop cost near the unguided baseline.

</details>


### [390] [RoboVista: Evaluating Vision Language Models for Diverse Robot Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04610)
*Shuangyu Xie,Kaiyuan Chen,Ziyang Chen,Simeon Adebola,Yixuan Huang,Zehan Ma,Tianshuang Qiu,Wentao Yuan,Dhruv Shah,Pannag R. Sanketi,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RQA评估框架和RoboVista基准（含474个VQA实例，覆盖39种机器人任务类型），发现当前VLM在机器人模块化推理中表现不足，且RoboVista性能与真实机器人任务执行强相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准大多基于遥操作端到端数据集，难以捕捉机器人行为所需的模块化决策结构（如感知、规划、控制等独立组件的推理）。需要一种能评估VLM在多样化机器人应用中模块化推理能力的框架。

Method: 提出RQA（Robot Question Answering）模块化评估框架，构建RoboVista基准（来自真实机器人系统、研究论文和专家标注），包含474个VQA实例、39种任务类型（农业、工业、家用、手术、自动驾驶等），评估多种SOTA VLM。

Result: SOTA VLM在RoboVista上存在显著性能差距；物理机器人实验表明RoboVista表现与真实任务执行高度相关。

Conclusion: RoboVista是一个有效的模块化VLM机器人推理评估基准，能反映真实部署能力，当前VLM在该基准上仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Diverse applications for robotics, such as industry and agriculture, require robots to operate across various embodiments, changing visual conditions, and complex planning. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer a promising foundation for general-purpose and interpretable robotic reasoning. Aligning VLMs with diverse robot applications requires a modular understanding of the individual decision components that underlie robotic behavior. Capturing such structure is challenging for conventional robot benchmarks that are primarily based on teleoperated, end-to-end datasets. We propose Robot Question Answering (RQA), a modular evaluation framework and RoboVista, a benchmark curated from real robotic systems, research papers, and expert annotations. RoboVista contains 474 Visual Question Answering (VQA) instances with human annotated reasoning and covers 39 unique task types in agricultural, industrial, domestic, surgical robotics, autonomous driving, and open robot datasets. Experiments on RoboVista show that state-of-the-art VLMs exhibit substantial gaps. Physical robot experiments suggest strong correlation between RoboVista performance and real-world task execution.

</details>


### [391] [DIVO: Continuous-time DVL-Inertial-Visual Odometry for Unmanned Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04615)
*Kyungmin Jung,Angad Bajwa,Junha Yoo,Arturo Del Castillo Bernal,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个基于高斯过程的连续时间轨迹估计框架，融合DVL、立体相机和IMU的异步测量，用于水下机器人的声-光-惯性里程计。采用学习型特征提取与匹配的视觉前端，在真实水下巡检数据上优于现有SOTA SLAM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境存在光衰减、光照变化和颗粒物等挑战，单靠视觉定位困难，需要融合多传感器模态并构建鲁棒的视觉跟踪管线。

Method: 基于高斯过程的连续时间轨迹估计框架，融合DVL、立体相机、IMU的异步测量；学习型视觉前端用于鲁棒特征提取与匹配。

Result: 在真实水下巡检数据集上，在精度、鲁棒性和轨迹覆盖率方面均优于现有视觉-惯性和声-光-惯性SLAM算法。

Conclusion: 提出的连续时间框架能无缝集成额外传感器模态，适应不同水下环境，无需重新配置，且仅依赖短期视觉数据关联即可达到SOTA性能。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel acoustic-visual-inertial odometry solution leveraging a continuous-time trajectory estimation framework for unmanned underwater vehicles. Underwater environments present unique challenges for visual localization and mapping, such as light attenuation, illumination variance, and the presence of particulate matter. This motivates the use of additional sensing modalities and a visual tracking pipeline that is robust to diverse subsea conditions. The proposed system is the first continuous-time trajectory estimation framework based on Gaussian processes to fuse asynchronous measurements from a Doppler velocity log, a stereo camera, and an inertial measurement unit. Additionally, a novel visual frontend is proposed, incorporating learning-based feature extraction and matching that is robust to the specific challenges that subsea environments present. The proposed framework enables seamless integration of additional sensor modalities in continuous-time and is adaptable to different environments without reconfiguration. The proposed system is extensively tested on real-world underwater inspection datasets, where it outperforms state-of-the-art visual-inertial and acoustic-visual-inertial SLAM algorithms in accuracy, robustness, and trajectory coverage. Notably, the proposed system outperforms the state-of-the-art despite only forming short-term visual data associations.

</details>


### [392] [SILO: Simulation-in-the-Loop Sim-to-Real Transfer for Multi-Stage Cable Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04616)
*Stone Tao,Jie Xu,Hesam Rabeti,Yashraj Narang,Yijie Guo,Iretiayo Akinola*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Sim-to-Real强化学习的多阶段电缆布线框架，利用GPU并行模拟训练策略以泛化到不同电缆几何形状，并设计了SILO执行框架和鲁棒状态估计来弥合模拟到现实的差距。真实实验中成功率更高且周期时间减半，是首个成功实现Sim-to-Real策略迁移的多阶段电缆布线工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法需要数千次示教且泛化性差，难以扩展到不同电缆类型和复杂变形场景，亟需一种可扩展、可泛化的线性可变形物体操作方案。

Method: 采用GPU并行模拟训练强化学习策略；提出SILO（Simulation In the LOop）执行框架将模拟作为闭环控制的一部分；结合局部RL策略和鲁棒电缆状态估计，实现Sim-to-Real迁移。

Result: 在真实世界电缆布线任务中取得了更高的成功率，且相比此前最优学习方法，周期时间减少了2倍。

Conclusion: 这是首个成功实现Sim-to-Real策略迁移的多阶段电缆布线工作，证明了大规模GPU并行模拟训练的策略可以泛化到真实世界中的多种电缆类型。

Abstract: Linear-deformable manipulation remains challenging due to the complex deformations of objects such as cables and ropes. Prior data-driven approaches, particularly imitation learning, have shown some promise in narrowly defined settings but typically require thousands of demonstrations for specific tasks and cable types, limiting scalability and generalization. We introduce a sim-to-real reinforcement learning (RL) framework for multi-stage cable routing that leverages GPU-parallelized simulation to approximate linear deformable behaviors. Training across thousands of parallel simulations enables the learned policies to generalize across diverse cable geometries and deformation patterns. To bridge the sim-to-real gap, we propose a novel deployment strategy that combines a Simulation In the LOop (SILO) execution framework, localized RL policies, and robust cable state estimation. On real-world cable routing tasks, our approach achieves higher success rates and 2x reduction in cycle times compared to prior state-of-the-art learning methods. To our knowledge, this is the first successful sim-to-real transfer of RL policies for multi-stage cable routing. Videos and additional visualizations are available at https://silo-cable-routing.github.io/

</details>


### [393] [Governed Caste Reassignment in Heterogeneous Swarms: An Asymmetric-Trust Protocol with Audited Operator Countersignature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04634)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种非对称信任协议，用于异构机器人群体中的角色重分配。自动收紧（降权）自动执行，松弛升权需操作员副署签名，并附带因果链日志。审计层使用Merkle哈希链日志和拜占庭容错复制，在100机器人规模下验证了安全性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构群体中角色重分配仅作为内部算法处理，缺乏外部授权和审计机制。对于实体部署，升权操作是治理事件，必须可审计且受外部权威控制。

Method: 提出非对称信任协议：自动收紧（降权）自动通过，松弛升权需操作员按预算副署签名。每次转换携带签名因果链，提交到Merkle哈希链审计日志，离线审计员仅凭操作员签名身份清单即可验证。审计层支持多副本拜占庭容错复制。

Result: 使用Ed25519签名在100机器人规模下：自动收紧在个位数到低两位数毫秒内完成；协议通过构造拒绝了四种攻击（角色洗钱、重复松弛升级、操作员冒充、因果链伪造）；部分治理基线隔离了每个门控阻挡的攻击；随机模糊攻击未发现漏洞。审计层在模拟和真实多进程TCP部署（100进程）中验证了共识和分叉排除。

Conclusion: 该协议将单智能体角色变异治理门泛化到群体级角色治理，首次为异构机器人群体提供了可审计、可外部授权的角色重分配安全框架。

Abstract: In heterogeneous robot swarms, caste reassignment (rebinding a robot to a new capability-bound role) is a high-frequency runtime event driven by battery, payload, and priority changes. Existing approaches treat it as an internal allocation algorithm and do not expose the reassignment to external authority. We argue that for regulated embodied deployments a caste change that elevates a robot's privilege envelope is a governance event that must be auditable and externally authorised. We propose an asymmetric-trust protocol: auto-tightening reassignments (to safer, lower-privilege castes) are admitted automatically, while bounded relaxation (to higher-privilege castes) requires an operator countersignature against a per-axis budget. Each transition carries a signed cause-chain, committed to a hash-chained Merkle audit log that an offline auditor verifies from an operator-signed identity manifest alone. We evaluate a reference implementation with real Ed25519 signatures over fleets up to 100 robots: auto-tightening completes in single-digit to low-double-digit milliseconds, and the governed protocol refuses four explicit attacks (caste laundering, repeated-relaxation escalation, operator impersonation, cause-chain forgery) by construction, with a partially-governed baseline isolating which gate stops which attack and a randomized fuzz adversary finding no admission. A distributed audit layer replicates the log across N per-member replicas with quorum-committed total order and cryptographic fork exclusion; we prove agreement and fork exclusion and validate them both in simulation and as a real multi-process deployment over TCP sockets (up to 100 real processes) with a Byzantine equivocator, on which every honest replica agrees, detects the equivocation, and commits no fork. The construction generalises a single-agent persona-mutation governance gate to swarm-level caste governance.

</details>


### [394] [KAM-WM: Kinematic Affordance Maps from Latent World Models for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04652)
*Xinyu Shao,Keru Zhou,Guowei Huang,Yajun Gao,Tongtong Cao,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KAM-WM从冻结的潜在视频世界模型中提取粗粒度的运动方向先验（Kinematic Affordance Map），无需展开或微调，通过Flow Matching图像到视频骨干的单步潜在速度生成交互区域和运动结构，压缩为token条件化扩散策略，在LIBERO和RoboTwin2.0上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本操作学习需要视觉先验同时捕捉"在哪里交互"和"如何开始交互"，现有的静态先验（如分割掩码）只编码前者，缺少运动方向信息。

Method: 使用冻结的潜在视频世界模型（Flow Matching图像到视频骨干），通过单步推理获得潜在速度，将其解释为运动学可供性图（KAM），提供任务条件化的交互区域和粗粒度运动结构；再用轻量级Perceiver将KAM压缩为token，与RGB观测和本体感受一起条件化扩散策略。

Result: LIBERO平均成功率90.6%；RoboTwin2.0 Easy 65.7%，Hard 22.4%。对比零阶掩码先验证明方向信息带来了增益。

Conclusion: 在评估设置中，冻结的视频模型可以在无需测试时展开的情况下为控制提供有用的一阶视觉先验。

Abstract: Learning manipulation from few demonstrations requires visual priors that capture not only where to interact, but also how the interaction should begin; static priors such as segmentation masks encode only the former. We present KAM-WM, a framework that extracts a coarse directional interaction cue from a frozen latent video world model without rollout or world-model fine-tuning. KAM-WM queries a Flow Matching image-to-video backbone once and interprets its single-step latent velocity as a Kinematic Affordance Map (KAM), which provides task-conditioned interaction regions and coarse motion structure. A lightweight Perceiver compresses KAM into tokens that condition a diffusion policy together with RGB observations and proprioception. Across LIBERO and RoboTwin2.0, KAM-WM reaches 90.6% average success on LIBERO and achieves 65.7% and 22.4% success rates in the Easy and Hard settings on RoboTwin2.0, respectively.
